**3. Nick Nurse — "His role has shifted a little bit for us this year and that one of the things at the top of his priority list is to supply energy." — Nick Nurse on the evolution of Tyrese Maxey's role** [06:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QVOwzm4j5-c&t=375s)

Nick Nurse: "His role has shifted a little bit for us this year and that one of the things at the top of his priority list is to supply energy. He's the youngest of the crew. He's got to have explosive energy at the start of most games. It's a little shift for him because he was playing super huge minutes and super huge ball in his hands, super huge chances last year. So, he doesn't have the ball in his hands as much, so he's got to go get it, he's got to find it, he's got to do all the other things like flying and rebound."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QVOwzm4j5-c&t=375s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QVOwzm4j5-c&t=375s)

**4. Jordan Ott — "It is just frustrating with the contact of a bump on a cross screen versus driving downhill." — Jordan Ott on inconsistent officiating and foul calls** [02:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3khZ-SuiYpQ&t=140s)

Jordan Ott: "It is just frustrating with the contact of a bump on a cross screen versus driving downhill appears to be different disruption. When you are just going to take a cut to get a catch versus downhill, we are top ten in drives, we are bottom five in free throws. So some of that, we definitely have to clean up, fouling the jump shooters, but trying to figure out what exactly pressure you can apply and which pressure puts you in a spot to where free throws are rewarded."

**5. Rick Carlisle — "Braden Smith was tremendous and showed the kind of competitor that he is." — Rick Carlisle praises Braden Smith's performance and competitive nature** [01:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=78s)

Rick Carlisle: "**Braden Smith** was tremendous and showed the kind of competitor that he is and has demonstrated over the past several years at Purdue. He was prepared for this opportunity because of all the work he put in the summer. When we did the beep test before training camp, he got to level 15 when everybody else bowed out at level 12. It just goes to show you that putting in that kind of work will bear fruit. It was inspiring to watch him battle tonight, and I thought defensively he did a great job too."

**6. Quin Snyder — "the paint is something that we've got to shore up one way or the other" — Quin Snyder on the need to improve paint defense** [02:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LAwNZJYlivQ&t=177s)

Quin Snyder: "The paint is something that we've got to shore up one way or the other, where you're kind of picking your poison at times, but when there's shooters on the court, that's probably the biggest thing for us right now defensively is just not doing a better job protecting the paint because it's going to be really difficult to win when teams are scoring in the paint like these guys did and some other teams have too."

**7. Shai Gilgeous-Alexander — "Just try to figure out what winning will look like with this team and this group." — Shai Gilgeous-Alexander on learning how to win with current roster** [02:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Nl1FvgRJrFs&t=160s)

Shai Gilgeous-Alexander: "My head now is just trying to figure out what winning will look like with this team and this group, as myself and the rest of the group have come so accustomed to what winning will look like with the whole group that we've had. So just trying to figure that out, and that's all that matters. Trying to win games and figure out what it looks like in the NBA, that gets to a team full of NBA players. Even when you might not have enough talent on paper what people say you still do, you just have to do it a different way, and we found that way tonight, and that's going to be the challenge going forward for the rest of the season."

**9. Mark Daigneault — "there's certain nights when you have a player like that where you just unleash them" — Mark Daigneault on Shai Gilgeous-Alexander's offensive performance** [00:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bzsDtldF50U&t=49s)

**22. Rick Carlisle — "It just illustrates the power of hard play and the power of hard unselfish play." — Rick Carlisle on the team's resilience despite injuries and suspensions** [00:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=0s)

Rick Carlisle: "I told the guys after the game that the night after the Palace brawl, there was a game very similar to this where we had six guys available due to suspensions and injuries. We went toe-to-toe with an Orlando team that was rested in this building with **Grant Hill**, **Tracy McGrady**, and a lot of other good players. It was a game almost identical to this. I told them that people are still talking about that game, and they will talk about this game for a long time because what those six guys did was very inspirational. It illustrates the power of hard play and the power of hard unselfish play and putting a real competitive spirit on a game."

Donovan Clingan: "I feel like it's just play hard, play for one another, and trust the coaching plan, trust the game plan. I feel like the coach, we have a great staff. ## Rick Carlisle, Braden Smith & Quenton Jackson Postgame Media Availability | November 2, 2026 — *Indiana Pacers*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I)

_Speakers identified: Rick Carlisle, Braden Smith, Quenton Jackson_

**1. Rick Carlisle — "It just illustrates the power of hard play and the power of hard unselfish play." — Rick Carlisle on the team's resilience despite injuries and suspensions** [00:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=0s)

Rick Carlisle: "I told the guys after the game that the night after the Palace brawl, there was a game very similar to this where we had six guys available due to suspensions and injuries. We went toe-to-toe with an Orlando team that was rested in this building with **Grant Hill**, **Tracy McGrady**, and a lot of other good players. It was a game almost identical to this. I told them that people are still talking about that game, and they will talk about this game for a long time because what those six guys did was very inspirational. It illustrates the power of hard play and the power of hard unselfish play and putting a real competitive spirit on a game."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=0s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=0s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=0&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=8_rJJTVEf0I&t=0&q=1&slack=1)</small>

Rick Carlisle: "**Braden Smith** was tremendous and showed the kind of competitor that he is and has demonstrated over the past several years at Purdue. He was prepared for this opportunity because of all the work he put in the summer. When we did the beep test before training camp, he got to level 15 when everybody else bowed out at level 12. It just goes to show you that putting in that kind of work will bear fruit. It was inspiring to watch him battle tonight, and I thought defensively he did a great job too."

## Cameron Boozer Press Conference | Grizzlies vs. Pelicans — *Memphis Grizzlies*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GIDZ04-GiV8](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GIDZ04-GiV8)

_Speakers identified: Cameron Boozer, Unidentified speaker_

**1. Cameron Boozer — "It's special just what Ty's doing this year and what he's been doing since he's got here." — Cameron Boozer on playing alongside Ty Jerome** [00:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GIDZ04-GiV8&t=12s)

**Unidentified speaker:** "Maybe I'm wrong, but I'm assuming you've been the best player on your team for most of your life. Have you ever played alongside a late-game player like **Ty Jerome** and what he was able to do? What is that experience like being on the court with him in those late moments?"

**Cameron Boozer:** "I definitely have. I think it's special just what Ty's doing this year and what he's been doing since he's got here. ## Ayo Dosunmu Postgame Interview With Sean Dorcellus | 11.02.26 — *Minnesota Timberwolves*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE)

_Speakers identified: Sean Dorcellus, Ayo Dosunmu_

**1. Ayo Dosunmu — "I'm just living in the moment trying to take each opportunity, each game for what it's worth" — Ayo Dosunmu on maintaining an aggressive mindset for the Timberwolves** [00:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=6s)

**Sean Dorcellus:** "19 points, 12 rebounds, 8 assists. What was your mindset coming into this one?"

**Ayo Dosunmu:** "Just being aggressive. I'm trying to get the win. We're short on guys right now, but I'm just living in the moment trying to take each opportunity, each game for what it's worth, and trying to get a win, trying to compete."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=6s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=6s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=6&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=6&q=1&slack=1)</small>

**2. Ayo Dosunmu — "We weren't fouling. I think in the first half we had a pretty good job defensively" — Ayo Dosunmu on the importance of defensive consistency** [00:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=20s)

**Sean Dorcellus:** "What made the difference in the second half for you guys to be able to pull away here?"

**Ayo Dosunmu:** "We weren't fouling. I think in the first half we had a pretty good job defensively, I think we only gave up 47 points. So just doing that more in the second half and then just not getting discouraged. Sometimes you look at a team record and the body can naturally just think that you're entitled to a win, but in the NBA, there's great teams, great players, and nothing is easy. So just going out there and just chopping wood each and every possession."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=20s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=20s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=20&q=2) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=20&q=2&slack=1)</small>

**3. Ayo Dosunmu — "Taking it one game at a time. Now we focus on Houston." — Ayo Dosunmu on taking the season one game at a time** [00:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P8JQQTSC0oE&t=45s)

**Sean Dorcellus:** "How do you keep chopping wood the rest of the week and turn this one win into a streak?"

**Ayo Dosunmu:** "Taking it one game at a time. Now we focus on Houston. Enjoy this win, get treatment, get proper rest, and then get ready for Houston, take each game at a time, and just keep getting better."

Ty Jerome: "I think just the possession game. We got to rebound the basketball, trim down some turnovers. They got 10 more shots than us and we lose by a possession. Jamahl Mosley: "We talk about by committee and it definitely was by committee. These guys did an unbelievable job of finding a way to stay poised. Our coaches did an unbelievable job of talking about the game plan and what we could give up, what we could take away. ## L'Equipe journalist Maxime Aubin on Victor Wembanyama dealing with the NBA Finals aftermath — *HoopsHype*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wr4wIbIRk8s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wr4wIbIRk8s)

_Speakers identified: Maxime Aubin_

**1. Maxime Aubin — "This is still something that hurts him, you know, what happened in the finals." — Victor Wembanyama's reaction to the criticism following the NBA Finals** [00:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wr4wIbIRk8s&t=0s)

Maxime Aubin: "After the finals, with all the criticism he had, I was really hopeful I could try to talk to him one-on-one, like a lot of other people. Let's say he was very quiet. So I talked to his people, to his agents, and they said basically he doesn't want to talk about the finals. From what I understand now, this is still something that hurts him, you know, what happened in the finals. He doesn't really want to talk about it yet."

Maxime Aubin: "One other thing I think that's happening right now for him is that he had a short summer. Mark Daigneault: "I thought we got off to a good start. I thought we really executed, which was a build on last game. I thought we executed pretty well last game, especially defensively, didn't show up in the outcome, but we built on that tonight, weather a really tough storm in the third quarter, and then got contributions from everybody that went in the game. Just great competitive effort, great togetherness, hung in there, and some cold-blooded shot-making at the end."

## Damian Lillard Postgame Interview | Portland Trail Blazers 118, Denver Nuggets 110 | Nov. 2, 2026 — *Portland Trail Blazers*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE)

_Speakers identified: Damian Lillard_

**1. Damian Lillard — "it was a game that we had to fight for" — Damian Lillard on the team's resilience and physical play** [00:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=0s)

Damian Lillard: "I liked that it was a game that we had to fight for. We came out and started the game and it seemed like we couldn't make a shot. We had balls rattling in and out, got good looks and we fought a lot. They were able to get out to a lead on us but we just talked about not allowing them to pick us apart for threes. We knew we would be able to get ourselves back in the game if we stayed physical and continued to play good offense and I think overtime that paid off for us."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=0s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=0s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=0&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=0&q=1&slack=1)</small>

**2. Damian Lillard — "there is no excuses, we are fouling" — Damian Lillard on reducing fouls and maintaining discipline** [00:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=41s)

Damian Lillard: "A lot of the plays in the first half, they probably had a quick whistle a little bit, but a lot of them I think we just made undisciplined plays. We were in a position where it could be a no-call and we took it a step further too many times where we made it look that way or probably did foul. That is what coach said at halftime. He said there is no excuses, we are fouling. He just got that out of the way and I think in the second half we kept up our physicality but we were much more aware of the moments that we were probably too aggressive or when we reached or got undisciplined and it hurt us and I think in the second half we were more aware of it and it didn't happen as much."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=41s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=41s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=41&q=2) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=41&q=2&slack=1)</small>

**3. Damian Lillard — "I told him, I want you to be the horse" — Damian Lillard on the team's chemistry and shared offensive responsibility** [01:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=101s)

Damian Lillard: "I think he has been himself. Coming in, I told him, I want you to be the horse. We said the same thing. The type of physicality he plays with offensively, how he can get in the paint, you almost have to foul him because he is that big and that fast and that forceful. That is good offense. Guys are going to have to help. You want to be able to get into that paint to get defenses to collapse and be able to play off of that on the perimeter, spray out passes. Once he gets stopped, there was a play down the stretch tonight, he attacked and they pulled all over and I was able to catch on the run and make a shot coming off that secondary action. Him knowing for sure that we are encouraging that, you be the horse, you be on go, you get downhill, you attack. I think that is good for our team."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=101s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=101s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=101&q=3) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=101&q=3&slack=1)</small>

**4. Damian Lillard — "how invested everybody is from top to bottom" — Damian Lillard on the team's collective investment and work ethic** [03:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eUP8JDDWCEE&t=213s)

Damian Lillard: "I would say what impresses me the most is how invested everybody is from top to bottom. Usually you get on a team and it will be some guys that might not get as many minutes or feel like they should get more minutes. Everybody is fully invested in doing what their job is for the team regardless of what that looks like. You look at **Chris Youngblood** tonight, this dude probably didn't even know he was going to be on this trip until the day we left. We played the game early Saturday, Sunday morning before we left, he got up and he worked out with me. That is how this team is. Everybody is on point and fully invested in their role and their job to this team."

## Full Denver Nuggets Postgame Media Availability vs. POR 🎙 — *Denver Nuggets*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM)

_Speakers identified: Coach Adelman, Nikola Jokic, Jamal Murray, Christian Braun_

**1. Coach Adelman — "It's one of the ways in the NBA you can lose a game." — Nuggets struggle with defensive rebounding and second-chance points** [00:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=13s)

Coach Adelman: "We gave up 16 on the glass. I also thought there were multiple times where we got our hands on it like they did, and then the 50-50 behind it, which leads to the layers of this. They shoot 15 more shots in this game. It always leads to an advantage for a team. We talked about it in the locker room, not to mention, they don't have a traditional four playing. So this was guards crashing all over the place. Obviously, **Klingan** does that, but I thought a lot of those were off other people's mistakes, where you don't win your own battle, and **Joker** is taking on somebody else in the rotation, and he ends up the benefactor of that, especially in the second half. It's one of the ways in the NBA you can lose a game, that you play so well to start and they claw back into it just by doing that."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=13s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=13s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=13&q=1) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=13&q=1&slack=1)</small>

**2. Coach Adelman — "People have to think that every night is going to be their night like he did." — Nuggets need better shot selection and trust in teammates** [01:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=87s)

Coach Adelman: "Those two guys tried to make plays that we need other creations for other people. You run something, if the ball finds you, there's a reason. They bring three people to two. I've said this before, have extreme confidence in all these guys. What **CB** did the other night in Phoenix, people have to think that every night is going to be their night like he did, as opposed to turning things down, driving the ball back into traffic. We ended up with 26 threes, but that's really a lie. I think we were at 14 middle of the fourth. I just thought we just, confidence has to be there. If those guys create it, you have to live off that and live with the result, as opposed to throwing the ball right back to them and now they're up against the clock taking tough shots."

[https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=87s](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=87s)

<small>[Clip it](presserclips://clip?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=87&q=2) · [Send to Slack](presserclips://clip?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=87&q=2&slack=1)</small>

**3. Coach Adelman — "We have to get more threes. We have to." — Nuggets need to improve interior scoring and three-point shooting** [02:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kdW3LWSq7bM&t=157s)

Coach Adelman: "We've always been the opposite analytic team. If you're efficient in the paint, you can get away with it. Especially to your point, because this would be a skewed night with the 34 free throws. You make 29, the majority are at the basket. But no, we have to get more threes. We have to. And like I said, if the creation leads to an open man, he has to shoot it. You can't drive it back in there. I thought we had multiple possessions late in the clock where we don't shoot it at seven seconds and you're down to the last three, four seconds. But just being reliance on yourself is really important because we're never going to shoot 50 threes in a game with the team we have. But we can complement the paint points to your point with free throws, and if we get to the mid-30s in threes, I think we're in a good spot."

## LIVE: Pelicans vs. Grizzlies Postgame Interviews 11/02/2026 — *New Orleans Pelicans*

Source: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=evHgO-CQ570](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=evHgO-CQ570)

**1. Jamahl Mosley — "These guys did an unbelievable job of finding a way to stay poised going into overtime." — Jamahl Mosley on the Pelicans' team-oriented approach to winning** [11:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=evHgO-CQ570&t=707s)

Jamahl Mosley: "We talk about by committee, and it definitely was by committee. These guys did an unbelievable job of finding a way to stay poised going into overtime. Obviously, we had the lead and gave it back to them, but the ability to find a way down the stretch to get the stops that we needed, to execute the things that we needed, that's what I'm most proud of these guys. The learning lessons that they're continuing to show and following the game plan. Our coaches did an unbelievable job of talking about the game plan and what we could give up, what we could take away. Obviously, there's always going to be cleanup in these situations, but the resiliency and the toughness and the grit that these guys showed, that's what it's got to look like."
